Deduplicate scenario commands already included by D.1, platform/artifact commands are gap-only. Workspace tier owns provider wire/dependency or compiling cross-crate semantic cutover, not precaution. | docs/research/pi-ai-opi-ai-alignment/acceptance-matrix.md#执行层次与流程同步要求 |

Reentrant gemini/google, vertex/google-vertex, bedrock/amazon-bedrock config/store reference migration detects conflicts before copy/ACK/switch and never overwrites accounts, rewrites historical ApiKind or retains permanent builtin aliases. Test profile-specific wire fixtures not factory count alone. Own the assembled full source closures for frozen route/effort (AIR-001/V-01), acquisition defaults and source send accounting (AIR-007/V-05/DEL-05/DEL-06), built-in catalog behavior (AIR-034/064/V-28/56), adapter-specific sampling/pricing (AIR-062/V-54), and transcript/native-effort stamps unaffected by sampling/onPayload overrides (V-16). All corresponding mechanisms are supplied by predecessor tasks. Reuse their named fixtures/results at the final relevant state and add only missing cross-path conformance; no second driver, catalog owner, retry coordinator or full implementation pass. Own complete V-32 through actual adapter capture -> terminal -> serialize/reload -> next request, including same-identity effort stamps and cross-identity projection; use the completed 20.7 replay mechanism and 20.37/protocol producers, never hand-constructed metadata alone.

Existing valid wire/safety cases stay, obsolete assumptions change only after replacement proof.

Before DoD / 原 DoD：

Verify full native option/default inventory from SDK annex§2, successful streaming/tool/usage and setup/HTTP/parser/callback/cancel/drop failures, original-schema validation and canonical replay. Existing valid wire/safety cases stay, obsolete assumptions change only after replacement proof.

Draft DoD / 草案 DoD：

Real adapter plus controlled transport proves lawful send/callback counts, owned failure/opaque data/duration and no new acceptance caps. Existing valid wire/safety cases stay, obsolete assumptions change only after replacement proof. For adopted Pi 1.3 options record source/type/unit/omission/default/false/zero/null/removal/precedence/wire/typed-rejection and capture actual public native/simple requests; reuse existing wire cases and add only distinct missing input/outcome cases. Preserve first-choice text/dataURL order, encoded original/decode status, text-only/empty success and skipped nonmatching entries, source600000ms body-inclusive timeout and base-price exception. Wrong operation/modality rejects before auth where known, no URL fetch/file write/image streaming/second protocol is invented. | docs/research/pi-ai-opi-ai-alignment/spec-draft.md#Delivery decomposition |

| public_seam_admission | Two real reference consumers, not two image protocols: .repo/pi-1.3.0/packages/coding-agent/src/extensions/codemode/execute.ts:765 (installed at507, delegated via core/model-registry.ts:187 and core/model-runtime.ts:802) and .repo/pi-1.3.0/packages/durable/src/codemode/models.ts:278 (installed codemode.ts:251). Both need typed model selection, authenticated dispatch, cancellation, structured output and preserved failure/usage. Shared conformance is planned at openrouter_fixtures.rs and image_provider_serialization.rs/public generate_images with OpenRouter real adapter and at external_sdk.rs for common consumption workflows. Those hosts are evidence only, no codemode/durable deliverable or invented second Opi consumer. Semantic necessity: closed operation/model identity prevents image being routed as chat/classifier or successful generation being reclassified by host plumbing. Product neutrality: no terminal/durable policy. Depth/locality: same owned auth/callback/cancel/result mechanism serves the two real reference use cases. Coupled lifecycle: API/model/options/typed result must evolve with this SDK binding. Mechanical verification: V21/22/23/25/26/42/50/58. Minimal authority: explicit call only, no extra destination/download/file/executor; native dependencies separately gated and not added by this image seam. Signed placement is this inferred case subject to human graph review and actual shared conformance, not Pi parity waiver. | docs/research/pi-ai-opi-ai-alignment/spec-draft.md#Phase delivery contract; docs/opi-spec.md#8. Before DoD / 原 DoD：

Preserve first-choice text/dataURL order, encoded original/decode status, text-only/empty success and skipped nonmatching entries, source600000ms body-inclusive timeout and base-price exception. Before DoD / 原 DoD：
Public classify reaches real llama-cpp-classify adapter with tokenization/template/logprobs/probability algorithms and actual image/refusal rules. Preserve native options/request/result/retry/timeout from SDK annex§4. Validate only source-required field/type/finite constraints, no universal probability range/sum/argmax/label membership rule. Typed invalid result retains known usage/metadata, cancellation and callback failure do not trigger hidden retry. One shared ClassifierApi conformance spans all four real protocols, not image adapter count.

Public classify reaches real llama-cpp-classify adapter with tokenization/template/logprobs/probability algorithms and actual image/refusal rules. Preserve native options/request/result/retry/timeout from SDK annex§4. Validate only source-required field/type/finite constraints, no universal probability range/sum/argmax/label membership rule. Typed invalid result retains known usage/metadata, cancellation and callback failure do not trigger hidden retry. One shared ClassifierApi conformance spans all four real protocols, not image adapter count. For adopted Pi 1.3 options record source/type/unit/omission/default/false/zero/null/removal/precedence/wire/typed-rejection and capture actual public native/simple requests; reuse existing wire cases and add only distinct missing input/outcome cases. llama-cpp classifier parameters and tokenization/template/logprob result preserve their own protocol and image/refusal rules.

This slice closes only the ordinary callback path; 20.11 consumes it and owns strict-variant interaction, while operation tasks 20.43-50 wire newly introduced APIs and 20.57 closes full AIR-059/V-51 coverage. | onboarding_acceptance | Supplement the existing owner with ONB-04 observable acceptance; retain its original scope, dependencies, history and protocol/version contract. Before DoD / 原 DoD：
Wire headers, onPayload, onResponse and raw-event callbacks through Models and every currently existing migrated real direct adapter at its source-defined timing. Public ordinary-call fixtures prove post-transform validation/protected identity, once-per-variant payload freezing across ordinary acquisition retry, raw-before-normalized order, functional callback failure/cancel and supported-versus-unsupported protocol behavior. This slice closes only the ordinary callback path; 20.11 consumes it and owns strict-variant interaction, while operation tasks 20.43-50 wire newly introduced APIs and 20.57 closes full AIR-059/V-51 coverage. Never recover raw events from a normalized EventStream or stack a second driver.

Draft DoD / 草案 DoD：
Wire headers, onPayload, onResponse and raw-event callbacks through Models and every currently existing migrated real direct adapter at its source-defined timing. Public ordinary-call fixtures prove post-transform validation/protected identity, once-per-variant payload freezing across ordinary acquisition retry, raw-before-normalized order, functional callback failure/cancel and supported-versus-unsupported protocol behavior. This slice closes only the ordinary callback path; 20.11 consumes it and owns strict-variant interaction, while operation tasks 20.43-50 wire newly introduced APIs and 20.57 closes full AIR-059/V-51 coverage. Never recover raw events from a normalized EventStream or stack a second driver. Explicitly prove SDK header omission/set/removal and protected selected identity through actual requests, with source-qualified per-variant callback timing/count and frozen ordinary retries. Runtime signal/fetch/functions are not JSON configuration. New operation consumers and 20.57 retain their full cross-operation closure.

New scenario / 新场景 `ONB-04-20.51`：
Explicitly prove SDK header omission/set/removal and protected selected identity through actual requests, with source-qualified per-variant callback timing/count and frozen ordinary retries. Runtime signal/fetch/functions are not JSON configuration. New operation consumers and 20.57 retain their full cross-operation closure.
